
#### slot / window — autumn-shoulder origins 2026-08-08..2026-10-07

common target window 2026-08-15..2026-10-07 (comparable with PAR-827's headline pass). Source: `paired_margin_window_slot.csv`. `margin < 0` means the candidate is better; a win requires the **park-cluster** interval to exclude 0.

`cov_ref` = paired rows / the baseline's own covered rows; `cov_model` = paired rows / the candidate's own covered rows. **BENCH-SPEC #8**: the publication gate needs BOTH ≥ 0.30, and a cell below it is *not tested*, not lost. `cov_ref` alone cannot see this — a thin baseline has a small denominator and scores near 1.00 while the pairing covers a sliver of the candidate.

#### intraday / window — autumn-shoulder origins 2026-08-08..2026-10-07

common target window 2026-08-15..2026-10-07 (comparable with PAR-827's headline pass). Source: `paired_margin_window_intraday.csv`. `margin < 0` means the candidate is better; a win requires the **park-cluster** interval to exclude 0.

`cov_ref` = paired rows / the baseline's own covered rows; `cov_model` = paired rows / the candidate's own covered rows. **BENCH-SPEC #8**: the publication gate needs BOTH ≥ 0.30, and a cell below it is *not tested*, not lost. `cov_ref` alone cannot see this — a thin baseline has a small denominator and scores near 1.00 while the pairing covers a sliver of the candidate.
